### Fixed

- Conftest isolation test fails in CI: inner pytest cannot import tests.\* (DEF-CI-BDRABCZUK-5DDFC7E2436D, task TASK-CI-BDRABCZUK-3C67155EA267)
- main.py console prints the raw CLI argument dict on every start and the update banner also with --generate-config (DEF-CONSOLEQ-BDRABCZUK-43110686358A, task TASK-CONSOLEQ-BDRABCZUK-3C57B917EF80)
- cortical_tiles parses subject IDs and output roots by string split and full-path regex (DEF-CORTILES-BDRABCZUK-9C7D2B9265BC, task TASK-CORTILES-BDRABCZUK-0316C777334E)
- cortical_tiles gives a subject the ICBM2009c transform of another subject whose ID it prefixes (DEF-CORTILES-BDRABCZUK-8A44DAD7F312, task TASK-CORTILES-BDRABCZUK-7924DB754D80)
- Stage 2 run_cortical_tiles logs a false ERROR 'Path does not exist: \<out\>/derivatives/pipeline_loop_2mm.json' in a nominal run (DEF-CORTILES-BDRABCZUK-C1A5989DEEA4, task TASK-CORTILES-BDRABCZUK-DE26A842B9E5)
- cortical_tiles select_good_qc drops subjects with leading-zero numeric ids (DEF-CORTILES-BDRABCZUK-777B67E5881A, task TASK-CTFAIL-BDRABCZUK-4B4C55276271)
- run_cortical_tiles skips regeneration when partial crops from a crashed run exist (DEF-CTFAIL-BDRABCZUK-E4A65DFD40E1, task TASK-CTFAIL-BDRABCZUK-540CFAFABCA0)
- cortical_tiles generate_distmaps globs the subject files with wildcards on both sides, so a subject id that prefixes another (sub-1, sub-10) can get the other subject distance map (DEF-CTFAIL-BDRABCZUK-23F5BA7BE2AF, task TASK-CTFAIL-BDRABCZUK-B0FECE6F754B)
- A subject whose fold-label generation fails aborts every region of the cortical_tiles crop stage (DEF-CTFAIL-BDRABCZUK-CB59D8EDF4AF, task TASK-CTFAIL-BDRABCZUK-DDD09F370348)
- test_suppr_benchmark fails intermittently: simple-surface choice depends on heap-address vertex order (DEF-CTILES-BDRABCZUK-E58AE8DAB15C, task TASK-CTILES-BDRABCZUK-326AD94FF0A0)
- cortical_tiles generate_skeletons counts the Morphologist-level qc/ folder and input_qc.tsv as subjects (DEF-INPUTKIND-BDRABCZUK-B02AC57FDC8E, task TASK-INPUTKIND-BDRABCZUK-288C29E2DE4A)
- A clean run exits 3 because the qc/ folder of the Morphologist tree is listed as a subject by stage 6 (and stage 2) input QC and excluded with an ERROR (DEF-INPUTKIND-BDRABCZUK-F68F83F3C40B, task TASK-INPUTKIND-BDRABCZUK-47531A068E4D)
- \--bids: Morphologist output has no t1mri folder, so stages 2+ find no scans, and the stage 1 and stage 2 participant ids disagree (DEF-INPUTKIND-BDRABCZUK-48F830CF3A22, task TASK-INPUTKIND-BDRABCZUK-526E0B196DDA)
- dataset.morphologist_graphs is both stage 1's output root and stage 2's subjects folder, so one config cannot chain raw T1s into stage 2 (DEF-INPUTKIND-BDRABCZUK-9555EBD838DB, task TASK-INPUTKIND-BDRABCZUK-73A867FCABC0)
- Wrong provenance: region_embeddings/provenance.json records a pipeline_commit that is not the commit of the code that ran (DEF-PROVENANCE-BDRABCZUK-C0FC651FAF1D, task TASK-INPUTKIND-BDRABCZUK-85EA0A72286A)
- README.md and docs/usage.md give .../morphologist-6.0/subjects as the only Morphologist layout, but the tree stage 1 writes has no subjects/ level and real existing trees do (DEF-INPUTKIND-BDRABCZUK-E09CDA22FFF2, task TASK-INPUTKIND-BDRABCZUK-9F0A13D462C4)
- prune_failed_subjects --qc with a --bids input_qc.tsv deletes every crop that has a session part, passing scans included (DEF-INPUTKIND-BDRABCZUK-0151BA26539C, task TASK-INPUTKIND-BDRABCZUK-A28E3B7AA790)
- \--bids whole-brain ventricle removal looks for the transform under a doubled acquisition (DEF-INPUTKIND-BDRABCZUK-6EEBFEA85344, task TASK-INPUTKIND-BDRABCZUK-ECE16A831BF5)
- is_non_subject treats a path with a trailing separator as a subject: is_non_subject(".../qc/") returns False and is_non_subject(".../.x/") returns False, while the same paths without the trailing separator return True (DEF-INPUTKIND-BDRABCZUK-14301060B97C, task TASK-INPUTKIND-BDRABCZUK-EF79D9CFBED9)
- Stage 2 logs one ERROR per subject when stage 1 ran without sulcal recognition (DEF-LABELQC-BDRABCZUK-7B7511AAF1A5, task TASK-LABELQC-BDRABCZUK-668C358EB42D)
- main.py crops with canonical_25 masks when --models-path is a local model set without model_set.json and no --masks-version: masks and models end up incoherent, caught only as a UserWarning in stage 4 (DEF-MODELVER-BDRABCZUK-5D21E2EA192C, task TASK-MODELVER-BDRABCZUK-0F461C8198D1)
- Local model path without --model-version silently reads canonical_25 crops (DEF-MODELVER-BDRABCZUK-B69D9AC66796, task TASK-MODELVER-BDRABCZUK-304F9E6E3EEC)

- prune_failed_subjects and purge_subject read only crops/2mm and raise ValueError on current output, which run_cortical_tiles writes under crops/\<masks_version\>/2mm (DEF-PATTERNS-BDRABCZUK-36F2AACA0EBF, task TASK-PATTERNS-BDRABCZUK-B87D0474A69C)
